                \texttt{Player B: Moving E left to its goal at (R5,C3). This opens (R5,C4), and I plan to move B down to (R5,C2) next. That will block the top{-}left corridor, so if your X at (R1,C2) needs to leave, please move it before then. E at (R5,C3) may block the direct row{-}5 crossing; the lower route remains open. PLAN: move E from (R5,C4) to (R5,C3) — REQUEST: please move X at (R1,C2) out before B takes (R5,C2)} \\
            }
        }
    }
    & & \\ \\

    \theutterance \stepcounter{utterance}

\end{supertabular}
